Aurelius Victor: Historia Romana (1871 Tauchnitz Latin edition)
Sextus Aurelius Victor

Source: Internet Archive — https://archive.org/details/historiaromanaa00victgoog
Edition: Tauchnitz stereotyped Latin edition (Leipzig: Otto Holtze, 1871), text following Gruner, via Internet Archive scan. Contains the full corpus: Origo Gentis Romanae, De Viris Illustribus, De Caesaribus, and the Epitome de Caesaribus. No public-domain English translation exists (Bird's 1994 Liverpool translation is in copyright); staged as the PD Latin documentary text.
Public domain. Staged for the Tower of Babel library, 2026-10-09.
